Track paragraph elements whose text/tails were modified, so we can remove layout caches underneath.\n    elem: ET.Element\n\n\ndef _iter_xml_files(root: Path) -> List[Path]:\n    return sorted([p for p in root.rglob(\"*\") if p.is_file() and p.suffix.lower() in {\".xml\", \".hpf\"}])\n\n\ndef _load_json() -> Tuple[Dict[str, str], List[str]]:\n    overview = json.loads(AUDIT_OVERVIEW_JSON.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n    summary = overview.get(\"summary\", {})\n\n    actions_doc = json.loads(CORRECTIVE_ACTIONS_JSON.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n    actions = actions_doc.get(\"immediate_actions\", [])\n    if not isinstance(actions, list):\n        raise ValueError(\"corrective_actions.json immediate_actions must be a list\")\n\n    #
